Proof 1: Ideal Gas Law: PV = nRT

Statement: Ideal Gas Law: PV = nRT

Hypotheses: ideal gas, low pressure, high temperature

Conclusion: PV = nRT

Definitions: pressure, volume, temperature, moles

Proof (Variation #1):

Step 1: Empirical law from gas experiments

Step 2: Avogadro: equal volumes, equal molecules

Step 3: Boyle: P*V = const at fixed T

Step 4: Charles: V/T = const at fixed P

Step 5: Combine into single equation

ID: fbdb8431a7e8 | Status: UNFORMALIZED | Complexity: O(n^2)

Mutation test: Generic existence theorem

Provenance: Domain Thermodynamics, theorem #2

Extra proofs (+1 from domain theorem bank)

Proof 1: Chemical thermodynamics: Gibbs energy determines spontaneity

Statement: Chemical thermodynamics: Gibbs energy determines spontaneity

Hypotheses: chemical systems

Conclusion: Delta G < 0 = spontaneous

Definitions: Gibbs energy, spontaneity, equilibrium

Proof:

Step 1: G = H - TS

Step 2: dG = VdP - SdT (const T,P)

Step 3: Delta G = Delta G^0 + RT ln Q

Step 4: At equilibrium: Delta G = 0, Q = K
